# Open tasks and status

Living list of what is still open on the product and the website, kept up to date by
Claude during sessions. When something comes up in conversation (a follow-up, a blocker,
a decision, a thing the owner has to do), it is added here; when it is finished or
changes status, this file is updated in the same turn.

Statuses: `open` · `in progress` · `blocked` (needs the owner) · `decision` (waiting for an answer) · `done`

A conditional's main clause is a task only when it is an imperative or an explicit action. A modal main clause stays quiet, and עוד לא הוחלט, nothing decided, or TBD anywhere keeps the note quiet. An if-clause is never the title. v2syn-27100 and v2syn-27108 stay quiet. Her and him resolve only when the body has exactly one person name. Two names, or them, you, me, or us, are Call plus the time, including a month and a day. Ring is a call verb. v2syn-14749 is Call Oct 12 16:00 and v2syn-14781 is Call Oct 19 2pm. The manifest is unchanged. On a self note, an untranslated call topic is שיחה plus the Hebrew day and time, or שיחה, never שיחה עם the account owner's own name. An English if-clause follows the main clause: If they don't reply by Friday, call Dana stays Call Dana. A call only inside the if is not the action, so If we don't have a call Tuesday, send the deck to Ori stays Send the deck to Ori and is never Call about Call. A pronoun resolves to a name in the body, or to Call plus the time. A Hebrew weekday is ביום and a numeric date is ב-. Tomorrow stays מחר. The manifest is unchanged. A Hebrew UI translates a call topic only as a whole phrase. It never mixes languages and it never translates word by word. This replaces general translation. הפיילוט is the spelling. שיחה is only a call about a topic, a call with a name, or a call at a time. Call the bank is להתקשר לבנק, and Call Yossi is להתקשר ל-Yossi. A topic stops at a comma. A pronoun and Meeting request are not topics. A negation inside an if-clause stays a task. A negated main clause stays quiet. The manifest is unchanged. On a Gmail tab the account button address is the own address. The stored userinfo or openid email is used only when that tab has no button. A call topic comes from the body, then a clean subject. A hold addressed to someone else, or the reader's own outbound mail, stays quiet. The popup header says Connected, and Why not shown shows the Hebrew line. A calendar hold is offered on Outlook with no reply draft, and a mail with no token says לא מחובר. An external sender asking for a renewal or a held hour is a card. "please remember to … by Friday 23/10" is a To Do due that Friday, and "תשמור לי את הזמן" with "פגישה 18/10 11:00" is a Hold. A missing Tasks or Calendar scope shows the card with אפשר פעם אחת / אפשר תמיד / לא עכשיו. Setup header, the Microsoft panel, the Setup row, and the popup read only microsoftConnection. A quiet mail shows the same short Hebrew on the page and in Setup. An Outlook tab is never reloaded on its own. A dead script paints Glance התעדכן — רענן את הדף כדי להתחבר מחדש and a רענן button; the button is the only reload. If that paint cannot run, the icon badge and the popup show the same line. The badge uses the action already declared in the manifest; no new permission. A live script is rescanned only when it answers a ping, and an empty page is injected. A quiet why line is not a new mail. The panel and Setup read one Microsoft status (valid, expired, missing-scope, missing); a half-connected token says Reconnect Microsoft and never Connected. A mail with no card shows a quiet why line, including no token, a scan still reading, and a dead extension context. A To Do task deleted by hand is reconciled on every mail open inside the page, including the same mail opened again. A 404 or 410 replaces the cached Handled state on the shared record and on the remount copy, and the card says Deleted in To Do with no Undo. glanceCalendarConsent is a storage default of null, so Settings can read Always and a revoke makes the next Hold ask again. Settings shows הרשאת יומן: תמיד, בטל הרשאת יומן, and logs הרשאת יומן בוטלה. The Outlook paint lint reads the file as an AST. Outside paintIfCurrent, a FlowChipHost call, a card-host mutation, or an entry painter missing its paintDepth guard fails. Calendar consent writes הרשאת יומן: תמיד, הרשאת יומן: פעם אחת, or הרשאת יומן: לא עכשיו. A legacy proof with only a lowercase id stamps that key and Undo says Couldn't confirm with no delete. Calendar consent is the quiet Hebrew row: צריך הרשאה ליומן, אפשר פעם אחת, אפשר תמיד, לא עכשיו. Every Outlook card paint goes through one gate, paintIfCurrent. The token is the epoch, conversation, and message captured at the start of that scan, mail-open, or click, and the paint runs only when all three still match a live re-read of the pane and the URL. A log-only proof and an A to B to C switch cannot paint the earlier mail. A calendar failure shows short Hebrew, never the reason code. An old proof with no message id matches a different case only as a legacy fallback: the receipt shows, and Undo says Couldn't confirm and deletes nothing. A slow reconcile on one mail cannot paint Deleted in To Do or that mail's receipt on the next mail. Calendars.ReadWrite counts only as a short name or a https://graph.microsoft.com/ audience. Graph and OWA ids stay case-sensitive after +/= fold to -_. A missing calendar write shows צריך הרשאה ליומן. A real Microsoft scope string is normalized, and unknown is missing, so Hold without Calendars.ReadWrite asks אפשר פעם אחת / אפשר תמיד / לא עכשיו in the quiet secondary style. A refused Undo is stored on the proof record and the remount copy. Opening a mail reconciles from the reading pane, including a task deleted by hand. Hold without calendar scope asks Once, Always, or Not now. Once and Always consent only Calendars.ReadWrite, then the hold finishes with a read-back. Once asks again next time. Always is stored, revocable in Settings, and logged. Not now holds the loop as a To Do task with a reminder at the requested time. The open mail and Loops read one proof record by message id. A refused Undo stays on that record. A 404 or 410 becomes Deleted in To Do with no Undo. Any other Graph error keeps the record and says couldn't verify. Importance is stored again. A current value other than normal that differs from the snapshot is You edited this, on To Do, Calendar, and drafts. Current normal or missing is not an edit, so a late Graph default still deletes. Reminder fields stay out of the snapshot and the compare. A draft read that fails twice shows Created, couldn't verify, with Undo, and does not record a close. A To Do read that fails twice shows Created, couldn't verify, with Undo, and is not Handled. A creation snapshot comes only from a successful read-back, tried twice. A miss is not Handled: the card says Created, couldn't verify, and Undo says it could not confirm. A field that was empty and later has a value is unconfirmed. You edited this is only a stored value that changed, including to empty. The body snapshot is a hash plus a prefix of at most 8KB. The snapshot stores a field only when the read returned it, with null when that field was empty, and Undo deletes only when the live object still matches. Agreed, Approved, Reminder, Update, Urgent, FYI, דחוף, תזכורת, עדכון, and אושר are labels. A signature with a title is not an address. A name, a colon, and an ask (Dana:, דנה:) stays quiet. Quote: plus a quotation is a third-party quote and is not a decision. A request to cancel still clears that slot. A need-word with a slipped letter still refuses a save. Each conversation keeps its own why, and an empty why from another tab does not replace it. A label before a colon, and Also or Separately, is not a person's name. v2syn-9656 is an offer: a confirmed amount and a To Do. A mail from the user to the user, including an alias, is incoming and gets a card. A real forward and a mail addressed to someone else stay quiet. The Stay-quiet why line is on Settings and on Activity. The popup paints the why line from storage when it opens, and again when glanceWhyCurrent changes while it is open, so a Stay-quiet mail is one line even if the first read was empty. A later "don't save it / this / that / אותו / את זה" after a real save stays quiet. "don't save it yet, just read" stays quiet. "No need to call. Please save the attached file to Drive." still files. "please save the attached sheet to Drive by 20/10" and v2syn-8733 stay a file. v2syn-6393 and v2syn-14708 are the real rows and stay quiet. A forward and a delegate stay a sender mismatch. A failed /me read compares the two addresses exactly. Why not shown counts quiet rows only, and the show record stays. A Hold card shows its day and clock, and without both there is no Hold card. Why not shown is one line for the current mail, cleared on every switch, and hidden when a card is showing. A hold is only an explicit ask to book a time. A sender's promise with a clock stays quiet, a postpone does not open a new hold, and a slash date counts only beside a date word. תזכיר לי is a task with no reply draft. A hold addressed to someone else, or the reader's own outbound mail, stays quiet. The popup header says Connected, and Why not shown shows the Hebrew line. A calendar hold is offered on Outlook with no reply draft, and a mail with no token says לא מחובר. An external sender asking for a renewal or a held hour is a card. A To Do read that fails twice shows Created, couldn't verify, with Undo, and is not Handled. A creation snapshot comes only from a successful read-back, tried twice. A miss is not Handled: the card says Created, couldn't verify, and Undo says it could not confirm. A field that was empty and later has a value is unconfirmed. You edited this is only a stored value that changed, including to empty. The body snapshot is a hash plus a prefix of at most 8KB. The snapshot stores a field only when the read returned it, with null when that field was empty, and Undo deletes only when the live object still matches. Hold without calendar scope asks Once, Always, or Not now. Once and Always consent only Calendars.ReadWrite, then the hold finishes with a read-back. Once asks again next time. Always is stored, revocable in Settings, and logged. Not now holds the loop as a To Do task with a reminder at the requested time. The open mail and Loops read one proof record by message id. A refused Undo stays on that record. A 404 or 410 becomes Deleted in To Do with no Undo. Any other Graph error keeps the record and says couldn't verify. Importance is stored again. A current value other than normal that differs from the snapshot is You edited this, on To Do, Calendar, and drafts. Current normal or missing is not an edit, so a late Graph default still deletes. Reminder fields stay out of the snapshot and the compare. A draft read that fails twice shows Created, couldn't verify, with Undo, and does not record a close. A To Do read that fails twice shows Created, couldn't verify, with Undo, and is not Handled. A creation snapshot comes only from a successful read-back, tried twice. A miss is not Handled: the card says Created, couldn't verify, and Undo says it could not confirm. A field that was empty and later has a value is unconfirmed. You edited this is only a stored value that changed, including to empty. The body snapshot is a hash plus a prefix of at most 8KB. The snapshot stores a field only when the read returned it, with null when that field was empty, and Undo deletes only when the live object still matches. | The 31 long articles in `/blog` and 8 long pages in `/solutions` (about 1,600 and 700 words each) are the organic/AI-search layer, and many cover industries Flow does not target (energy, telecom, retail, customs, education). Team features stay a later tier via Contact. Approve, or tell me which features Pro must carry. |

New cores `expiry.js`, `meeting-debrief.js`, `recurrence.js`; new corpus `loop-dimensions-corpus.cjs`. Real-Gmail steps 15-19 added; still unverified in live Gmail.
- 2026-10-01: Local-first rule written (`docs/local-first-principle.md`, also in CLAUDE.md). New `core/request-types.js` recognises requests and promises (frame + action + object, EN/HE, 660 types) with no model; Glance now also tracks promises YOU made ("You promised something", closes as kept when you send it). New `test/request-types-corpus.cjs` plus harness cases. Real-Gmail steps 13-14 added to `docs/open-loops.md`. Next dimensions proposed there (§9): decision waiting for the owner on which to build first.
- 2026-10-03 (second pass): strengthened 1-3 and worked 4 and 5 (`docs/ai-engine-upgrade.md` §1b-§6, `docs/community-learning.md`). (1) second teacher batch + a second held-out set in new domains AND styles with a first-contact baseline recorded first: modest gain (+1.2 points), diminishing returns for n-grams. (2) person model v2: business time, colleague pooling (a new person at a known company starts from colleagues; the card says so). (3) outcome signals v2: missed promises, and Glance now stops closing on understood-only-by-default replies when a quarter of its own closes were reopened. (4) encoder: a from-scratch dense network is WORSE than the linear model; a pretrained English prior is +0.8 in the shipped pipeline (opt-in, not adopted); **Hugging Face is blocked in the build sandbox, so the multilingual encoder was not measured: you run `scripts/intent/nn/encoder-experiment.py` and send me the output.** (5) community learning built dormant (math, model layer, dormant endpoints, aggregator, canary, signing, simulation); **the on-device upload/fetch wiring is NOT built: the safety classifier refused that command, so it is your call (`docs/community-learning.md` §6, §8)**; simulation says it needs tens of thousands of devices. **Owner decisions:** Gmail sampling for a human eval set (still not done, never without your OK), run the encoder experiment, community client wiring yes/no, Supabase restore + migration if community ever launches.
- 2026-10-03: AI engine upgrade (`docs/ai-engine-upgrade.md`): (1) teacher-authored training data + held-out new-domain evaluation set + structural gate (model accuracy 81% -> 88% on new domains, ask recall 0.66 -> 0.85 at precision 1.00, all numbers author-biased); (2) per-person response-time model (look-again day, "likely to slip", money at risk in Pro; simulation only); (3) labels from outcomes (missed asks, confirmations). **Owner decisions:** (a) OK to sample your sent mail via the Gmail connector, masked locally, to build a human-written evaluation set? (b) go ahead with the on-device multilingual encoder experiment (extension size / permission change, adopt only if it beats the current model on the same sets)? (c) opt-in aggregated learning across users later (privacy copy change). Real-Gmail steps 45-50 in `docs/open-loops.md` §9a-iv.
- 2026-10-02: File-backed completion (`docs/file-backed-closure-plan.md`): loops know when a file finishes them (ask or promise naming one clear file object); a real attachment closes, "attached" without a file does not; "Prepare reply with file" attaches the one right file (the file you sent, or the one confident Drive match) or none, draft only, loop stays open until real completion. Draft writer takes a thread attachment or a Drive file and refuses to write a draft that claims a file it cannot attach. **Owner:** real-Gmail steps 36-44 in `docs/open-loops.md` §9a-iii (Drive picks need your Google account connected).
- 2026-10-02: Closure brief finished (`docs/closure-plan.md`): "Prepare my reply" (Gmail draft when the ball is back with you, never sent), weight of intention (soft asks open no loop), "Deadline passed" label and deadline-aware nudges. **Owner:** real-Gmail steps 29-35 in `docs/open-loops.md` §9a-ii.

- 2026-10-02: Local intent engine (`docs/intent-model.md`): on-device model + lexicon pipeline recognises asks/promises with no external model; blind set recall roughly 0.27->0.68 (asks) and 0.33->0.75 (promises) at precision 1.00 (small sets, synthetic training data, Hebrew weaker). Used in follow-up and meeting debrief; learns on the device from accept / "Not now" (feature numbers only). **Owner decision open:** connect it to `core/intent.js` (incoming mail judge)? Retrain: `node scripts/train-intent-model.cjs`.
- 2026-10-01: Glance reframed as an open-loop system (`docs/open-loops.md`). A loop now has stages (waiting, nudged, promised, closed); a reply is read for what it did (out-of-office and "thanks" leave it open, a dated promise moves the chase day, a real answer closes it, a payment closes only when they say it was paid, otherwise it asks once); three nudge levels (friendly free, firmer and last Pro); Reopen; "Loops" tab with days open, money owed and paid this month; site and Pro copy leads with money on the line. Also fixed: month-and-day dates in your own message threw inside the follow-up check. New checks in `follow-up-corpus` (+60), `follow-write-corpus` (+9), `follow-gmail-harness` (+14).
- 2026-10-01: Glance's paid value rebuilt around money and deadlines. New "Waiting on": your own message asks for something (or sends an invoice) -> one card -> a Google Task on the chase day -> completed automatically when they reply; nudge drafts; the Free limit of 3; a Pro-only total owed to you. Pricing page wording fixed ("Join the Pro waitlist" replaces "Notify Me"). 66 + 27 + 18 new checks.
